2026-10-25T13:23:23.8172809Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Advance Decode IWG weight TMA, add sampled routing capture, and serve iteration 2 with in-job selection

The independent-WG loader currently waits for activation arrival before issuing
any weight transfer. Restore overlap for the first stage of each Decode task:
wait for its empty stage, reserve B transaction bytes and issue B before polling
L1 arrival or the FC2 mask. Keep the single full-barrier arrival with A and SFA;
subtract the already-reserved B bytes for stage zero and do not transfer B twice.
Leave weight-scale __ldg operations on the math path. The remaining stages retain
the existing empty-barrier pipeline and Prefill takes the unchanged branch.

Keep M1 as a kernel-bench candidate rather than enabling it in serving. Replace
the rejected Prefill bulk-scatter candidate, retain the final control repeat,
and add balanced 176 plus Decode balanced 128/160/176 and local 128 timelines.
The campaign has seven steps, including independent numerical gates and timing
runs. Timing covers pre-dispatch and MegaMoE together; instrumented timelines
remain separate from adoption measurements.

Add an opt-in Decode target-worker routing probe at the physical top-k boundary.
A device gate selects histogram writes inside graph replay, while forward hooks
sample only an explicit active window at a bounded interval. Mask padded rows
with the live device scalar, retain source EP rank and layer identity, and write
bounded per-step JSONL records into the requested run directory. Combine all four
source ranks on CPU to recover destination expert row counts, rank loads and
experts that need more than one M64 pool block. Keep the probe disabled by default
and use a separate diagnostic run when enabled because it adds kernels and host
copies. Restrict the sbatch integration to the Decode worker's optional env.

CUDA 13.0.88 CPU compilation on Firmus confirms 168 registers and unchanged 8-byte
spill stores/loads for control and M1; the initial spill-increasing formulation
was discarded. Prefill SASS is identical. Control/M1 timeline variants compile.
CPU checks cover capture enablement, windowing, bounds, source-rank aggregation,
barrier accounting and the actual histogram kernel in the Triton interpreter.
GPU correctness, performance and official serving results remain unverified.
No GPU jobs, Actions, commits, pushes or branch operations were performed.

This commit also builds the image and starts the one serving run for
iteration 2 on top of the 9022.10 line (run 40175897453). The new kernels
are chosen at job start by the existing gates and selectors, so no separate
probe or kernel bench runs first:
- Prefill FA3: the joined identity-PRMT PV variants (N224 regs232, two N192
  register allocations) and the corrected c2 V image join the fixed Prefill
  table. e4 and lazy are no longer offered.
- Decode FA3: the merge visibility fix is in the 16-entry candidate table.
- Decode dense: DG1 SwapAB stream-K is a dense-selector candidate.
- Decode norm: the one-row add-RMSNorm group-quant overlay. It must pass the
  exact-byte provisioning gate before serving starts.
- Decode admission cap rises to 176, with graph batches 42/44.
- Per-node power and clock telemetry is recorded at 1 Hz.
The M1 weight-first MegaMoE loader stays a kernel-bench overlay and is not
served. Routing capture stays disabled. The profile is off.
